- **delegate**: Delegate work to an external agentic CLI (OpenAI Codex, Claude Code, Gemini, Ollama — anything registered in crossmodel), spending that provider's quota instead of this session's. Three uses: (1) REPO SWEEPS — pass a directory and it greps and reads the tree itself: "where is X", "which files touch Y", "is this pattern used anywhere else". (2) IMPLEMENTATION in an isolated scope, with --write, ideally a git worktree. (3) Volume work from a spec — generate code, convert data, classify batches, second opinion. It never commits; review and commit stay with the caller. ([devloadingbr/claude-code-crossmodel](https://github.com/devloadingbr/claude-code-crossmodel/blob/HEAD/agents/delegate.md) · ★1)
- **delegate**: Research, sweeps, reviews and artifact work sent out of the main conversation. One call per batch of independent tasks rather than several sequential calls. ([rsotor/profesor-kit](https://github.com/rsotor/profesor-kit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/delegate.md) · ★1)

- **delegate**: Hand a self-contained coding, review or investigation task to Codex, Antigravity, OpenRouter or local Ollama, picked by live usage, to save Claude quota. Use when told to delegate, or when Claude's usage is high and a task can run without this conversation. - **delegate**: The agent an owner's one-off delegated work runs as — research, an investigation, a design or plan, a bounded change. Carries no posture, no loader and no inbox — the task prompt is the whole assignment. - **delegate**: Hands one task to another CLI harness (opencode, omp, pi, zcode, claude and any other in the huddle table) in the current folder (or the one `--dir` names) and returns the harness's report unchanged. - **delegate**: Delegate subagent for code and helper tasks during a `/astack:mode` run. Use for delegates, not as the runner for `/astack:mode` itself. - **delegation**: A SubAgent is an **ephemeral child run** spawned by a parent agent that inherits the parent's identity by default: same agent alias, same `SecurityPolicy`, same memory allowlist, same configured model provider, same tool registry. Auditable as a child via a tracing span `agent.&lt;alias&gt;.subagent.&lt;run_id&gt;`. ([zeroclaw-labs/zeroclaw](https://github.com/zeroclaw-labs/zeroclaw/blob/HEAD/docs/book/src/agents/delegation.md) · ★34k · 2 copies)

- **delegation**: How work is split between the session the user is talking to, the top level, and the agents it spawns. The split is shaped for the user's hands, not for an agent's throughput, and every rule below follows from that. Reads a Jira issue through whichever Jira MCP server is connected (or a local Markdown requirements file), normalises it into `.enabler/runs/&lt;KEY&gt;/requirements.json`, and judges whether the ticket is ready to be implemented. Read-only towards Jira and the codebase. Use it from the ai-enabler skills; do not use it to edit tickets. ([grananda/ai-enabler](https://github.com/grananda/ai-enabler/blob/HEAD/plugins/ai-enabler/agents/delivery-ticket-analyst.md))
- **delivery-ticket-refiner**: Refines a ticket the way a product owner and a business analyst would before a sprint - finds what the definition leaves unsaid (unhappy paths, limits, permissions, data, scope) and closes each gap as a marked addition, an assumption or a question. In the delivery pipeline it runs after the delivery-ticket-analyst and strengthens `requirements.json` before planning; stand-alone it writes an improved ticket as a local Markdown file from a Jira issue, a requirements file or a short brief. Never writes to Jira and never changes the meaning of what the author stated. ([grananda/ai-enabler](https://github.com/grananda/ai-enabler/blob/HEAD/plugins/ai-enabler/agents/delivery-ticket-refiner.md))
- **delivery-tracker**: Tracks what is actually done. Use after an item is verified (or at any time for a status report) to reconcile the roadmap, AGENTS.md "Where we are" and docs/STATUS.md against evidence — git history, test results, the qa-verifier's report — mark items done, keep Verified / Not verified honest, and produce a progress summary for the human. ([Shafichariri/kinspector](https://github.com/Shafichariri/kinspector/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/delivery-tracker.md) · ★1)

Use proactively before handoff, before release, after scaffold completion, and after remediation rounds. ([mitekk/codeViber](https://github.com/mitekk/codeViber/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/delivery-validator.md))
